# SmartRail — AI Interaction Log

This log is a required assessment deliverable (see `final-assessment-scoped 2.md`, sections 21–23
and 27). It records, turn by turn, which AI tools and models were used, what they were asked to do,
how their output was validated, and every case where AI output was accepted, rejected, or
significantly modified, with reasons.

One row is added per user request (per turn), not one summary per session, so conversational detail
is preserved. Maintained by the agent per the `ai-usage-log` skill. Earlier rows are never rewritten
or deleted.

| # | Date | Request | Model / agents | AI responsibilities & output | Validation | Accepted | Rejected / modified | AI gaps / my judgment |

Cause: SseStream.OpenAsync sends with ResponseHeadersRead, but under WebApplicationFactory's TestServer the response only reaches the client when the server writes its first body byte; JourneyStreamResult writes nothing until an event or a heartbeat, so on a quiet stream at the default 15 s heartbeat OpenAsync itself blocks about 15 s before the flow can publish, and a clock started before the open reads about 15 s plus about 1 s. A resumed stream writes its catch-up frame as the first byte, so the same clock reads about 1-2.5 s, which is exactly the asymmetry QA saw. The live path delivers in about 1 s | Rejected suspects (a) outbox signal running before commit, (b) consumer failing on first delivery with backoff, (c) response buffering in the writer: no src change made, since dispatcher pickup and hub delivery both happened about 1.07 s after the 201 and the delivery row showed as delivered on attempt 1.
